## Current implementation

- `src/engine/EngineB1.cpp`, `EngineState.h`, `Engine.h`: native peer render export/install/commit at completed boundaries. Exact float rows and FPBO SH codes/bounds cover inactive rows and the partial codec tail. Validate payload and lineage before writes; verify peer readback and owned/non-render identity afterward. Parent commit digest is part of payload identity.
- `src/app/TrainerCore.cpp`, `src/engine/EngineCheckpoint.cpp`: persistent worker barrier; no independent live-context autosave or uncommitted final checkpoint. STOP/CANCEL intent is preserved when a barrier is interrupted.
- `src/app/B1Group.cpp/.h`: opt-in `--view-policy full --refresh-cadence N`, immutable v3 group, attempt-bound exchange, export/install/save/commit/release, committed-pair-only recovery. Retain compact generation lineage and the latest pair; prune obsolete new attempt payloads/checkpoints only after replacement commit. Both old and new pairs still coexist during publication.
- `src/checkpoint/B1Contract.cpp/.h`, `B1Merge.cpp`: version-3 refresh bindings, mixed/stale/partial refusal and merge only at a committed generation. Historical v1/v2 semantics retained.
- Tests: `worker_request_test.cpp`, `checkpoint_resolution_test.cpp`, `b1_merge_contract.cpp`, `g1_native_proof.cpp`, `cli_training_smoke.cpp`.
- Usage: [B1 execution record](multi-gpu-training-b1-execution.md), [testing](../testing.md).

No numerical kernel, loss, optimizer, precision or quality tolerance change is intended. No commit or push was performed in this session; carry the working-tree changes, not just this document, to the next session.

Portable code handoff: `code-handoff.zip` in the local evidence bundle contains
`working-tree.patch` against base commit
`8f8c0f5a58c4740824ef5e9d4a335614424e4860`, the new handoff document, and a
hash-checked snapshot of the affected files. The patch includes all tracked
working-tree changes, including pre-existing changes; it is not a per-session
attribution diff. Do not blindly apply it over another dirty checkout.
It does **not** include the dataset, optimizer checkpoints or historical evidence:
preserve their manifest-bound dependencies separately.

## Original paused-handoff evidence

Local evidence bundle: `a-settlement-20261002T154816Z` under the requested opencode temporary directory. Its `campaign.manifest.json` and final evidence index bind paths, commands, hashes and results; private paths/device identities remain there.

| Check | Observed result |
|---|---|
| Final Windows dev Vulkan build, GUI off, patented off | Passed |
| Final headless CTest | 17/17 passed |
| Native `--b1-refresh-check` on AMD | Passed: 576 rows, 64-row tail, inactive row, exact peer installation and owned/non-render preservation; stale/hash/parent/truncated refusal |
| Real two-AMD persistent group, source step 4, horizon 64, eight updates/owner, cadence 2 | Completed four generations through step 12; merged publication; only latest checkpoint pair and payload pair retained |
| Resume already completed group | Refused with exit 2; completion bytes unchanged; no retraining |
| Final STOP before first generation commit | Exit 42, no completed publication; resume completed with exit 0 |
| Final STOP after first generation commit | Exit 42; resume completed with exit 0 |
| Final attempt-owned worker kill at next refresh boundary | Exit 2, sibling stopped, no completed publication; resume completed with exit 0 |

These are **short synthetic/native workflow checks**, not full-source A2 admission, long recovery, decoded `1e-4` recovery parity, quality acceptance or speed measurements. The standalone native proof uses its existing ordered edge fixture; it is not evidence of equivalence for the kitchen's unordered trajectory. Group fault runs use the ordinary CLI fixture.

Retained failing-before evidence includes:

1. New header inclusion leaked the Windows `near` macro into `Knn.h`; removed the Windows-bearing include from `B1Group.h`.
2. Policy serialization selected `emit(bool)` for a `const char*` ternary, breaking all contract schemas; explicit `std::string` fixed it. Existing contract tests caught the regression.
3. An interrupted barrier reset `save_on_stop`, producing worker exit 43 and group exit 2 for cooperative STOP. Removing the override preserves STOP/CANCEL intent without writing uncommitted state. Final before/after-commit STOP and resume checks passed.
4. The first proof invocation used hyphenated Vulkan UUID display text. Native selectors require `uuid:` followed by **32 lowercase hex digits without hyphens**.

## Original paused-handoff inputs and storage

Before implementation, all 60 retained protected bindings matched. All 1,416 files under the selected encoded input/sparse directories were hashed, including all 1,195 named training and 132 named held-out images; the two named view sets are disjoint. Original step-10,000 optimizer source and 30,000 horizon remain bound. Two AMD Radeon AI PRO R9700 devices were present; external device-local budget was 33,250,242,560 bytes each. External budgets are admission evidence, not worker accounting.

At handoff, all 1,416 encoded inputs and the 59 protected bindings other than the
expected rebuilt executable remained SHA-identical. Final executable SHA-256:
`1a585daabd66232ebbe67096da4857078124ab919ebf3c179e4b169bed79a7e3`.
No native training/proof process remained running. Small workflow runs did not
sample aggregate host RSS; their device-backing logs are not a full-source peak.

The explicit cleanup removed only 65,855 bytes of regenerable Python bytecode. Lossless filesystem compression of 22 checkpoint archives and 32 PLY files reclaimed **1,765,384,786 allocated bytes**; every file's before/after SHA-256 matched and original paths remain usable. Cleanup record: `cleanup-20261002T154243Z/cleanup.manifest.json`. Large G1/B2/Q/D/V files were retained because they are referenced evidence, not disposable cache.

Full-source work was **not admitted**. A conservative first matched-phase ledger reserves 4 GiB of new live storage: retained unchanged control, old/new treatment checkpoint pairs, old/new exchange payloads, publication temporaries and evidence margin. With the unchanged 10 GiB free floor this requires **14 GiB (15.03 GB) free before launch**. Free space was initially about 12.8 GB and rose to 14.43 GB at handoff, still below that bound. This is a planning bound, not a measured full-source peak. Only one writable local volume was found; the others were full optical volumes. Refresh resources before continuing; do not assume compression or delete protected evidence to meet the bound.

The bounded event comparison is exercised, but matching a retained native prefix is not contemporary timing/variability evidence or full-horizon coverage proof.

The source-check extension is a **raw installation discriminator**, not a trained trajectory, renderer equivalence or quality check. The source-scale group and sampled resource results are **bounded qualification checks**, not a comparable-quality speed result, true machine-wide peaks or long recovery qualification. Cadence 8 was diagnostic only. Historical Q's failed decoded `1e-4` recovery screen is unchanged; native remains the practical default.

The partial-install predicate is deliberately narrower than a native crash between installation and local commit: one owner's installation/local-commit acknowledgement was already present, the peer remained suspended and the paired commit was absent. Only the identified attempt-owned victim process tree was killed. `A2-partial_install.run.json`, `A2-partial_transfer.run.json` and their resume records bind the faults and successful recovery from original source, not authority from an uncommitted pair.

Owner-0's first resume exposed a coordinator defect: normal terminal `Stopped` was interpreted as an explicit owner STOP, returning 42 rather than publishing completion. `src/app/B1Group.cpp` now recognizes that condition only for the exact owner job explicitly requested to stop. The failing first resume is retained; `A2-owner0-stop-resume-repaired.run.json` records publication exit 0, and the fresh owner-1 STOP/resume records also finish at 0. Final application SHA-256: `c66ccb8088b46e3e08f592d6b08068fb0c9ca8942b07bd26f70783fc86708963`. `A2-final-headless.json` binds that application to the final 17/17 pass after the repair (7.99 s, no exclusions); earlier checks retain their recorded binary identities.

`A2-paired-state-invariants.json` and `A2-mixed-no-replace-refusals.json` bind the four final-pair invariants and refusal results. Native raster statistics (`eng.radii`) are outside the frozen-context invariant; this is not a claim that every saved byte equals source. New raw group data was archived losslessly and reconstructed/hash-verified, with native pair readback verified before redundant raw data removal. Preserve each `.compact.zip`, its compact record and the bound `compact-baseline`/original-source dependencies together; these are dependency-bound archives, not standalone checkpoints. Historical protected evidence was not removed.

`A2-native-eval.run.json`/stdout and `A2-native-mesh-input.run.json`/log record the native consumers above. The eval used the original source and its recorded earlier application binary; it is not a final-binary or refreshed-treatment quality qualification.

`A3-frozen-contract.json` predeclared cadence **1,000**, ten finite generations from source **10,000 → 20,000 under horizon 30,000**, before either arm. The measured approximately 21.3–21.94 s source barriers motivated a tenfold shorter maximum stale-context interval than unchanged 10,000-update full-view blocks without checkpoint/restart cycling. Approximately 220 s barrier wall was a planning estimate; the primary measured ledger below supersedes it, not the frozen endpoint or decision rule. This remains a diagnostic choice, not a selected winner.

The first A3 control's Python monitor exited with code 130 while the native coordinator and both workers continued without a training restart. Independent supervision was reattached, and a native process-lifetime observer was started. The intervening resource-sampling gap is **unverified**; do not relabel it as uninterrupted monitoring or A5 complete-cost qualification. The local `A3-admission.json`, input reverification, declarations and monitoring records preserve this distinction.

Both primary arms exited **0**, each with one uninterrupted native attempt and **10,000 accepted native events / 123,433,689,024 pixels per owner**. Each owner covers all **1,195 training IDs** and exactly matches the retained native step-10,000–19,999 input/face/dimensions/pixel sequence. Each split arm therefore has **20,000 aggregate exposures / 246,867,378,048 pixels**, versus native N's 10,000 exposures; this is not equal aggregate work. Source-relative clocks, immutable masks/configuration, source-exact shared and unowned optimizer/non-render state, and complete unique original-row coverage passed. Refresh additionally has **ten committed generations** and exact final paired render rows/FPBO SH codes/bounds across 1,000,000 rows, 3,907 blocks and the 64-row tail. Native raster statistics and scheduled camera/SH state are outside the frozen-source invariant; this is not all-byte source identity or decoded recovery parity. `A3-full-control.closed-receipt.json`, both arms' `.verification.json` and `A3-refresh1000.run.json` bind these observations.

`A3-quality-summary.json` records fresh unchanged native evaluations on the same **132 named held-out slots** for control, refresh and historical native N; all 1,416 bound encoded inputs were independently reverified before evaluation. The six-metric means are:

| Mean metric | Unchanged full-view control | Refresh 1,000 | Native N |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Raw PSNR (dB) | 20.1452341 | 20.1918447 | 20.7651462 |
| Raw SSIM | 0.8009832273 | 0.8006396970 | 0.8079750530 |
| Raw L1 | 0.0823940386 | 0.0829790432 | 0.0786941848 |
| Color-corrected PSNR (dB) | 24.2488492 | 24.3842932 | 25.2906076 |
| Color-corrected SSIM | 0.8345973712 | 0.8347551364 | 0.8422223636 |
| Color-corrected L1 | 0.0414641068 | 0.0406944311 | 0.0353173780 |

Refresh improves mean raw/cc PSNR over control by **0.0466106 / 0.1354439 dB**, descriptively closing **7.5189% / 13.0015%** of the mean native gaps; these are unclipped ratios of aggregate mean gaps, not average per-view closure. **Raw SSIM and L1 worsen**, while all three cc means improve. All six refresh means remain worse than N; residual raw/cc PSNR is **−0.5733015 / −0.9063144 dB**, with **101 / 128** regressing views versus N (**67 / 65** versus control). Worst PSNR residuals versus N are raw slot **118**, `IMG_2170.jpg`, **−2.9379 dB**, and cc slot **5**, `IMG_1707.jpg`, **−2.8657 dB**.

`A3-worst-render-verification.json` binds all six selected-view native GT/render runs at slots 5 and 118, each exit 0 with all **792 metric values** exactly matching its primary arm's complete 132-view evaluation. GT PNG bytes match across arms within each slot. Parent inspection of downsampled GT/N/control/refresh displays found apparent illumination, texture/reflection and edge differences, not a full-resolution quality screen, corrected-PNG comparison, root cause or full meshing. `A3-postrun-input-verification.json` records all **1,416 encoded input sizes/hashes unchanged** after these runs.

`A3-completion.json` closes the diagnostic and binds the executed next decision: residual six-metric loss and mixed response do not justify blind cadence refinement or A5. First forward RGB/depth/transmittance and native pixel cotangents were byte-exact; earliest reduction/screen-gradient differences were the same order as repeated all-owned native-control variation, **not a demonstrated mask-semantic bug**. Event-8 source-frozen-peer render/data-gradient differences were much larger than native repeat variation, supporting coupled-trajectory/staleness investigation, not attribution of A3's long residual or Q's failure. Descriptive `decoded_errors=0` does **not** establish numerical exactness or waive the unchanged `1e-4` screen. References freeze shared/topology and unmask Gaussian FPBO only, not ordinary whole-trainer equivalence.

The parent reports corrected mechanism wall **67.249 s**, sampled RSS **3,666,817,024 B**, new-live maximum **6,699,348,639 B** and free minimum **18,172,334,080 B**; sampled resources are not true peaks. ## Reproduce the bounded checks

**Historical protocol only.** These commands and fixture paths record earlier
bounded checks; they do not admit the selected original recordings or authorize
new evaluation, dataset redirection or an SDK-budget reset.

Use the current repository build scripts. These commands need two supported non-NVIDIA devices; `DEVICE_A` and `DEVICE_B` stand for their canonical selectors.

```bat
build_develop.bat -DSS_BACKEND=vulkan -DSS_BUILD_GUI=OFF -DSS_ENABLE_PATENTED=OFF
cmake -E chdir build_vulkan ctest -L headless --output-on-failure --no-tests=error -j 2
build_vulkan\g1_native_proof.exe --b1-refresh-check DEVICE_A
```

For the resumed source-check extension, use a symbolic original native optimizer checkpoint and canonical device selector:

```text
g1_native_proof.exe --b1-refresh-source-check --resume CHECKPOINT --device uuid:A
```

This performs raw installation/refusal checks without optimizer updates or a synthetic re-render. The resumed build and final current-build integrated headless rerun passed as recorded above.

Run the fixture command from a **new isolated working directory**, using absolute executable paths and `SS_TEST_DEVICE=DEVICE_A`:

```text
cli_training_smoke.exe ABS_SPIRULA_EXE --refresh-fixture
```

It writes a real 576-row native checkpoint at `training-fixture/outputs/direct/step-000000004.ckpt`, plus `training-fixture/views.txt`. Use absolute fixture paths:

```text
spirula train-blocks --resume SOURCE4 --views FIXTURE_VIEWS --devices DEVICE_A,DEVICE_B --output NEW_GROUP --updates 8 --view-policy full --refresh-cadence 2
spirula train-blocks --resume-group INCOMPLETE_GROUP
```

The local run records bind the actual fault commands and injection predicates. Workers/coordinator need writable stdin for `STOP`. Original tiny checks injected STOP when generation-6 owner readiness or generation-6 commit appeared, and killed an observed attempt-owned worker when generation-8 readiness appeared. Resumed actual-source checks used generation-10,004 readiness/commit and the partial-install/transfer predicates recorded above; they did not kill unrelated processes. Long A3 jobs belong to the monitored continuation, not the original no-running-process handoff; consult their run records before launching another GPU candidate.
